# The date patterns and zone data of the reference browser's ICU (Chrome 156,
# ICU 78, CLDR 48), read from its icudtl.dat: locale, then = and the
# locale it is an alias of, ^ and its parent where that is not its
# truncation, or P (DateTimePatterns), T (their atTime forms), A
# (availableFormats), N (appendItems) or I (intervalFormats, a skeleton
# and the greatest different field) with a key and a pattern; a locale
# holds what differs from its parent; E (eras) and D (flexible day
# periods), M (am and pm) with a width and a name, Z (zone formats).
# W and O (weekday and month names, by context and width) where ICU4X
# lacks them; a language@calendar for a default calendar not gregorian.
# * Z: a language whose zone names are English, or a locale whose are
# root's (none). * H: a region's preferred and allowed hour formats
# (timeData); * R: a language's day period, from and before (at,
# before empty); * L: a zone's region and 1 where it is the region's
# primary zone; * M: a zone's metazone from and to (UTC); * G: a
# metazone's reference zone for a region. Y: a name ICU4X has otherwise,
# a metazone's or a zone's (by type: l or s, then s standard, d
# daylight, g generic; empty for none) or a region's (C/). Non-ASCII
# as \uXXXX.
